How answers are graded:
How answers are graded

- Each attempt is compared with your answer and marked right or wrong. The grade is on the whole attempt; the final answer shown is picked out automatically as a guide.
- Numbers match when they are the same value written differently: 0.675, 67.5% and 67.5 percentage points all match, and so do different scales (thousand, million, billion), thousands separators, currency symbols and numbers written as words.
- If the question asks for a particular format (a percentage, a number of decimal places, a unit, a rounding or an order), the attempt must meet it. If it asks for none, any equivalent form is accepted.
- For an ordered list, a title, honorific or article added to an entry is accepted if the order is right either with it or without it.
- Only the value the attempt finally commits to counts. Values reached while working, or considered and set aside, do not. An attempt that offers several values without choosing one is wrong, even if one of them is right.
